\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a})\equiv\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\mathit{a}
.

Given:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}):T_{m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\>\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\mathit{a}:T_{m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:T_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}
and
m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.

Since
\mathbf{T}
is a functor,
\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

Therefore,
\llbracket\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a})\rrbracket=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

•

(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a})\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f}\equiv\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f})
.

Given:
\Gamma\vdash(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a})\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f}:T_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f}):T_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:T_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{A}\to T_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}
and
m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

Now,

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt\llbracket(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a})\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket\mathbf{join}^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{f})\>(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a}))\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mu^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{f})\>(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mu^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{f}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=\mu^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=\mu^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=\mu^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ(\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}\times\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle.

Next,

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt\llbracket\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{a})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{a}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle.

The morphisms above are equal because the diagram below commutes:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}\times\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces t^{\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{T}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}

The leftmost square commutes because
\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
is a
strong
natural transformation; the middle one commutes because
\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
is a natural transformation; the rightmost one commutes because
\mu
is natural in its first component.

\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b})\equiv\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{b})

Given:
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}):T_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}):T_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}

\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:T_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}
and

Now,

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt\llbracket\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\llbracket\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}))\>\mathit{a})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}))\>\mathit{a}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle.

Next,

\displaystyle\hskip 10.0pt\llbracket\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}))\>\mathit{a})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle.

The two morphisms above are equal because
\mu
is natural in its second component.

(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a})\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m}\mathit{f}\equiv\mathit{f}\>\mathit{a}

Given:
\Gamma\vdash(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a})\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m}\mathit{f}:T_{m}\>\mathit{B}

\displaystyle\llbracket(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a})\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m}\mathit{f}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{join}^{1,m}((\mathbf{lift}^{1}\mathit{f})\>(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}))\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{1,m}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{1}\mathit{f}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle\mu^{1,m}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{T}_{1}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{1}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{1,m}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{T}_{1}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{1}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ(\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}\times\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle.

And,

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{f}\>\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle.

The above two morphisms are equal because the diagram in Figure
19
commutes. The left square commutes because
\eta
is a
strong
natural transformation; the square to the right commutes because
\eta
is a natural transformation; the triangle to the right commutes because
\mathbf{T}
is a lax monoidal functor. Also, note
t^{\textbf{Id}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 5.0pt[\because\mathbf{T}\text{ is a lax monoidal functor}].

Figure 21

Commutative diagram

The diagram in Figure
20
commutes: the leftmost square commutes because
\mu^{m_{1},m_{2}}
is a
strong
natural transformation; the middle one commutes because
\mu^{m_{1},m_{2}}
is a natural transformation; the rightmost one commutes because
\mathbf{T}
is a lax monoidal functor.

Next, to show that the above morphisms are equal, we just need to show that:

t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\Big(\text{id}\times(\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\Big)\circ\langle\pi_{1},\text{id}\rangle=\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\pi_{1}\times\text{id})\circ\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\langle\text{id},\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

These two morphisms are equal by the commutative diagram in Figure
21
. The diagram in Figure
21
commutes: the bottom subfigure commutes by naturality of
t^{\mathbf{T}_{m_{1}}}
; the right one commutes by properties of products; the left one commutes by the commutativity of the diagram in Figure
18
.

∎

Appendix B
Proofs of lemmas/theorems stated in Section
4

Theorem B.1 (Theorem
4.1
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in GMC(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\overline{\mathit{A}}
in DCC(
\mathcal{L}
). Further, if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in GMC(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\simeq\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}
in DCC(
\mathcal{L}
).

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. By IH.

•

Rule
M-Return
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}:T_{\bot}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\overline{\mathit{A}}
. Therefore,
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\bot}\overline{\mathit{a}}:\mathcal{T}_{\bot}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}
.

•

Rule
M-Fmap
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}\mathit{f}:T_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}\to T_{\ell}\>\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{f}}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\to\overline{\mathit{B}}
.

Now,

\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}.\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y}):\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\to\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{B}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y}):\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{B}}\lx@proof@logical@and\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}},\mathit{y}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y}):\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{B}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}},\mathit{y}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y}:\overline{\mathit{B}}\ell\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{B}}\ell\sqsubseteq\ell

•

Rule
M-Join
. Have
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}:T_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:T_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>T_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}
.

Now,

\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\lx@proof@logical@and\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\lx@proof@logical@and\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}},\mathit{y}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}},\mathit{y}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathit{y}:\overline{\mathit{A}}

The above derivation uses the judgements
\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}
on the first and the second applications of the bind rule respectively.

•

Rule
M-Up
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}:T_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:T_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By IH,
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}
.

Now,

\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\lx@proof@logical@and\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\overline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathit{x}:\overline{\mathit{A}}\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}

.

Now, for
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
, if
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, then
\lfloor\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\rfloor
and
\lfloor\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\rfloor
are equal as
\lambda
-terms. Hence,
\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\simeq\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}
in DCC.
∎

Appendix C
Proofs of lemmas/theorems stated in Section
5

Theorem C.1 (Theorem
5.1
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in GCC, then
\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
. Further, if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in GCC, then
\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
.

Proof.

Follows from Theorem
A.1
by duality.
∎

Appendix D
Proofs of lemmas/theorems stated in Section

Theorem D.1 (Theorem
6.1
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in GCC(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\overline{\mathit{A}}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
). Further, if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in GCC(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\simeq\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
).

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
. Only the cases
\mathbf{extr}
and
\mathbf{fork}
are new; for the other cases, follow the proof of Theorem
B.1
.

Appendix E
Proofs of lemmas/theorems stated in Section
7

Theorem E.1 (Theorem
7.1
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in GMCC, then
\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
. Further, if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in GMCC, then
\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
.

Proof.

Let
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
. Then, by Theorems
A.1
and
C.1
,
\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
because a strong monoidal functor is also a lax and an oplax monoidal functor. For
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
, if
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, then
\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket
by Theorems
A.1
and
C.1
and the equations listed in Section
7.2
.

∎

Theorem E.2 (Theorem
7.2
).

Given any preordered monoid
\mathcal{M}
, for typing derivations
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
), if
\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket
in all models of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
), then
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is derivable in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

Proof.

We use standard term-model construction for proving this theorem. First, fix the preordered monoid,
\mathcal{M}
. Next, construct the freely generated bicartesian closed category,
\mathds{F}
, from the syntax of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
), as follows:

\text{Obj}(\mathds{F}),A,B::=\mathbf{Unit}\>|\>\mathbf{Void}\>|\>\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}\>|\>\mathit{A}+\mathit{B}\>|\>\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}\>|\>S_{m}\>\mathit{A}
.

\text{Hom}_{\mathds{F}}(\mathit{A},\mathit{B})=\{t\>|\>\emptyset\vdash\mathit{t}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}\}/=_{\beta\eta}
.

The objects of
\mathds{F}
are the types of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
) while the morphisms are the terms of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
) quotiented by
\beta\eta
-equivalence. This is the classifying category of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

Now, we define a strong monoidal functor
\mathbb{S}
from
\mathbf{C}(\mathcal{M})
to
\mathbf{End}_{\mathds{F}}^{\text{s}}
.

\mathbb{S}(m):=\mathbb{S}_{m}
where
\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{A}:=S_{m}\>\mathit{A}
and
\mathbb{S}_{m}(A\xrightarrow{t}B):=S_{m}\>\mathit{A}\xrightarrow{\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{t}}S_{m}\>\mathit{B}
.

\mathbb{S}(m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}):=\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{A}:=\lambda\mathit{x}:S_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}
.

Need to check that
\mathbb{S}
is well-defined. In other words, need to show that
\mathbb{S}_{m}
s are strong endofunctors and
\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
s are strong natural transformations.

\mathbb{S}_{m}
is a functor because:

For
A\in\text{Obj}(\mathds{F})
,
\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\text{id}_{\mathit{A}}=\mathbf{lift}^{m}\text{id}_{\mathit{A}}=\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{x})=\lambda\mathit{x}:S_{m}\>\mathit{A}.\mathit{x}=\text{id}_{S_{m}\>\mathit{A}}
.

For
f\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{F}}(\mathit{A},\mathit{B})
and
g\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{F}}(\mathit{B},\mathit{C})
,

\mathbb{S}_{m}\>(\mathit{g}\circ\mathit{f})=\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\mathit{g}\circ\mathit{f})=\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{g}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x}))=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{g})\>((\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{x})=\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{g}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{f}
.

Now we define strength of
\mathbb{S}_{m}
,
t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}
.

We have,
\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\times S_{m}\>\mathit{B}\vdash\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}):\mathit{B}\to\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}
.

Then,
\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\times S_{m}\>\mathit{B}\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})):S_{m}\>\mathit{B}\to S_{m}\>(\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B})
.

And,
\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\times S_{m}\>\mathit{B}\vdash(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}):S_{m}\>(\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B})
.

So,
\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}):\mathit{A}\times S_{m}\>\mathit{B}\to S_{m}\>(\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B})
.

We define:
t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}\triangleq\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})
.

Check that
t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}
is natural in both
A
and
B
:

The left diagram in Figure
22
commutes because:

\displaystyle t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A^{\prime}},\mathit{B}}\circ(\mathit{f}\times\text{id})

\displaystyle\;\;\mathbb{S}_{m}\>(\mathit{f}\times\text{id})\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A^{\prime}},\mathit{B}}\>((\mathit{f}\times\text{id})\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\mathit{f}\times\text{id}))\>((\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A^{\prime}},\mathit{B}}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\mathit{f}\times\text{id}))\>((\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathit{f}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathit{f}\times\text{id})\>((\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathit{f}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

The right diagram in Figure
22
commutes because:

\displaystyle t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B^{\prime}}}\circ\text{id}\times\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{g}

\displaystyle\;\;\mathbb{S}_{m}\>(\text{id}\times\mathit{g})\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}

\displaystyle\mkern-28.0mu=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B^{\prime}}}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{g})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\text{id}\times\mathit{g}))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle\mkern-28.0mu=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>((\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{g})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\text{id}\times\mathit{g})\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})\>\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle\mkern-28.0mu=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>((\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{g})\>\mathit{z}))\>\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\text{id}\times\mathit{g})\>(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle\mkern-28.0mu=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>(\mathit{g}\>\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\text{id}\times\mathit{g})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle\mkern-28.0mu=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{g}\>\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{g}\>\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathit{A}\times S_{m}\>\mathit{B}}

Now check that
t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}
satisfies the axioms for strength.

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B})\times\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{C}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m}\>((\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B})\times\mathit{C})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathit{A}\times(\mathit{B}\times\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{C})}

Commutative diagram

The left diagram in Figure
23
commutes because:

\displaystyle t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}\times\mathit{C}}\circ(\text{id}\times t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{B},\mathit{C}})\circ\alpha^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}\times\mathit{C}}\>(\text{id}\times t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{B},\mathit{C}})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}\times\mathit{C}}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{w}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{w})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{w}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z}))\>(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{w}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{w}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{w})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{w}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{w}))))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\alpha^{-1}\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B},\mathit{C}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\alpha^{-1}\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{z})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y})))\>(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y},\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z}))))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}).

The right diagram in Figure
23
commutes because:

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\pi_{2}\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\top,\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{z})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y})\>(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathit{z})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\pi_{2}.

So
\mathbb{S}^{m}
is a strong endofunctor.

Now, we need to check that
\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is a strong natural transformation. To show this, we shall use the following equality:

(19)

\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f}=\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 10.21385pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 11.67874pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 10.21385pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 11.67874pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m,1}(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x})))\>\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m,1}(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z})\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x})))\>\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m,1}(\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{x})\>\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m,1}(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 10.21385pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 11.67874pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{y}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation (\ref{eq:idr})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f}

As a side note, we can dualize the above argument to show that:

(20)

\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f}=\lambda\mathit{y}.((\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{f}\>(\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\ll\!\!=^{m}\mathit{y})

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces S_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces S_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces S_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{B}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces S_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{f}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{f}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}}

\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\text{id}\times\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}}

Commutative diagram

The left diagram in Figure

commutes because:

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{f}\circ\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f})\>(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{z}))\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation (\ref{eq:natl})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{f}

The right diagram in Figure

commutes because:

\displaystyle t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}\circ\text{id}\times\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>((\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z}))\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation (\ref{eq:natl})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>((\lambda\mathit{w}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{w}))\>\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{w}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{w})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}}\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}

This shows that
\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is a strong natural transformation. Hence
\mathbb{S}
is well-defined.

Next we show that
\mathbb{S}
is indeed a functor.

We have:
\mathbb{S}^{m\leq m}_{\mathit{A}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m,m}\mathit{x}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}=\text{id}_{\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{A}}
.

And,
\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\mathit{x}=\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}
.

Hence,
\mathbb{S}
is a functor. Next we show that
\mathbb{S}
is strong monoidal.

Define:
\eta:\textbf{Id}{}\to\mathbb{S}_{1}
and
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\to\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
as:

\eta_{\mathit{A}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}
and
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}
.

Need to check that
\eta
and
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
are strong natural transformations.

The diagrams in Figure
25
commute because:

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{1}\>\mathit{f}\circ\eta_{\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle\;\;\;\;t^{\mathbb{S}_{1}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}\circ(\text{id}\times\eta_{\mathit{B}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{1}\mathit{f})\>(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{1}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{y})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>((\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathit{B}}\circ\mathit{f}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}=\eta_{\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}}

This shows that
\eta
is a strong natural transformation.

Next, we show that
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is a strong natural transformation.

Note that

(21)

\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y}=\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{a})=\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y})\>\mathit{a})=\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}

Now, the left diagram in Figure
26
commutes because:

\displaystyle\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{f}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f}))\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{z}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y})\>\mathchoice{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 26.45134pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 27.91623pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{z})\hskip 2.0pt[\text{By Equation (\ref{eq:assoc})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 26.45134pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 27.91623pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f})\>(\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{f}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}

The right diagram in Figure

commutes because:

\displaystyle\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y})\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}})\>(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{z},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{z}))\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y})\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}})\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y})\>(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}})\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{z})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y})\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y})\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{u}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{v}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{u},\mathit{v})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{u}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{v}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{v})))\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{v}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{v})))\>\mathit{z}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathit{z}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{u}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{u}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 26.45134pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 27.91623pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{u}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{u})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation (\ref{eq:assoc})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 26.45134pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 27.91623pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{u}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{u})

and

\displaystyle t^{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\mathit{A},\mathit{B}}\circ\text{id}\times\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{z},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x},\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is a strong natural transformation.

Next, we show that
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is natural in both
m_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
m_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}

\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}}

Commutative diagram

The diagrams in Figure
27
commute because:

\displaystyle\mu^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle\;\;\;\;\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y})\hskip 2.0pt[\text{By Eqn. (\ref{eq:natl})}]

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y})\hskip 2.0pt[\text{By Eqn. (\ref{eq:natr})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{A}}

This shows that
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is natural in both
m_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
m_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Now we show that
\mathbb{S}
satisfies the axioms for lax monoidal functor.

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{1}\>\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathbb{S}_{1}\>\mathit{A}}

\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{1,m}_{\mathit{A}}}

\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\eta_{\mathit{A}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{m,1}_{\mathit{A}}}

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\>\mathit{A}}

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\>\mathit{A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\>\mathit{A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}}
