\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}}

Figure 28
.
Commutative diagram

The left diagram in Figure
28
commutes because:

\displaystyle\mu^{1,m}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\eta_{\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle\;\;\mu^{m,1}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m}\>\eta_{\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{1,m}(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m,1}(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 4.48613pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 7.47655pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}{\kern 8.94144pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{1}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y})

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 7.59612pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 10.21385pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}{\kern 11.67874pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m}}}}\gg\!\!=^{1}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y})\>\mathit{x}\hskip 2.0pt[\text{By Eqn. (\ref{eq:idl})}]

\displaystyle=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\hskip 2.0pt[\text{By Eqn. (\ref{eq:idr})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}=\text{id}

\displaystyle=\text{id}

The right diagram in Figure
28
commutes because:

\displaystyle\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\mathit{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\lambda\mathit{y}.(\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{w}.\mathit{w}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 24.8729pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 26.45134pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 27.91623pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathit{z}\hskip 2.0pt[\text{By Eqn. (\ref{eq:assoc})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 11.49895pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 14.11668pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 15.58157pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{w}.\mathit{w})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}},m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}_{\mathit{A}}\circ\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\>\mathit{A}}

Hence
\mathbb{S}
is a lax monoidal functor.

Now
\mathbb{S}
is also a strong monoidal functor because
\eta
and
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
are isomorphisms.

Define:
\epsilon:\mathbb{S}_{1}\to\textbf{Id}{}
and
\delta^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\to\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\circ\mathbb{S}_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
as:

\epsilon_{A}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x}
and
\delta^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{A}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{fork}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}
.

By dualizing the arguments presented above, we can show that
\epsilon
and
\delta^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
are strong natural transformations.

Further,
\eta
and
\epsilon
are inverses of one another because
\eta_{A}\circ\epsilon_{A}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x})=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
and
\epsilon_{A}\circ\eta_{A}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{extr}\>(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x})=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

Similarly,
\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\delta^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
are inverses of one another.

Hence,
\mathbb{S}
is a strong monoidal functor.

As such,
\llbracket\_\rrbracket_{(\mathds{F},\mathbb{S})}
provides a sound interpretation of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
). This is the generic model of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

Now we show that if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
), then
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{F},\mathbb{S})}=\lambda x.b\{\text{proj}_{i}^{n}\>x/x_{i}\}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{F}}(\Pi A_{i},B)
where
\Gamma=x_{1}:A_{1},x_{2}:A_{2},\ldots,x_{n}:A_{n}
and
\Pi A_{i}=A_{1}\times A_{2}\times\ldots\times A_{n}
. For
n\in\mathbb{N}
and
1\leq i\leq n
,
\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>x
is defined as:

\displaystyle\mathbf{proj}_{1}^{1}\>x

\displaystyle=x

\displaystyle\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{2}\>x

\displaystyle=\mathbf{proj}_{i}\>x\hskip 3.0pt\text{for }i=1,2

\displaystyle\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n+1}\>x

\displaystyle=\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>(\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{x})\hskip 3.0pt\text{for }2\leq n\text{ and }1\leq i\leq n

\displaystyle\mathbf{proj}_{n+1}^{n+1}\>x

\displaystyle=\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{x}\hskip 3.0pt\text{for }2\leq n

The proof is by induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. Standard.

Rule
MC-Return
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{b}:S_{1}\>\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{b}\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
.

Now,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{b}:S_{1}\>\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\eta_{\mathit{B}}\circ\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{b})\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
.

•

Rule
MC-Extract
. Similar to rule
MC-Return
.

•

Rule
MC-Join
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}:S_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:S_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{b}\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
.

Now,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}:S_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\cdot m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\mu^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}\circ\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{join}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b})\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
.

Rule
MC-Fork
. Similar to rule
MC-Join
.

•

Rule
MC-Fmap
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f}:S_{m}\>\mathit{B}\to S_{m}\>\mathit{C}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{f}\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
.

Now,

\displaystyle\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f}:S_{m}\>\mathit{B}\to S_{m}\>\mathit{C}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\mathbb{S}_{m}\>(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\Gamma,\mathit{B}}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbb{S}_{m}\>(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbb{S}_{m}}_{\Gamma,\mathit{B}})\>(\mathit{y},\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{w}.(\mathit{y},\mathit{w})))\>\mathit{z}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket)\>(\mathit{y},\mathit{z}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{u}.(\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket\>\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{u})\>(\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{u}))\>(\mathit{y},\mathit{z}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket\>\mathit{y})\>\mathit{z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket\>\mathit{y})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{lift}^{m}(\mathit{f}\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{m}\mathit{f})\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}

•

Rule
MC-Up
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}:S_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:S_{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{B}
and
m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{b}\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
.

Now,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b}:S_{m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\mathbb{S}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathit{B}}\circ\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{up}^{m_{{\mathrm{1}}},m_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{b})\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
.

Hence,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{F},\mathbb{S})}=\lambda x.b\{\text{proj}_{i}^{n}\>x/x_{i}\}
.

Now, let
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{B}
such that
\llbracket\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket
in all models of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
). Then
\llbracket\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{F},\mathbb{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{F},\mathbb{S})}
. Hence,
\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\{\mathbf{proj}_{i}^{n}\>\mathit{x}/\mathit{x}_{i}\}
. Therefore,
\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

This completes the proof.
∎

Theorem E.3 (Theorem
7.3
).

The generic model satisfies the universal property.

Proof.

Let the parametrizing monoid be
\mathcal{M}
. For GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
), let
\mathit{Cl}
be the classifying category and let
\mathit{G}
be the generic model.

First, we show how to define models of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
) in other categories using models of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
) in
\mathit{Cl}
. Let
\mathds{D}
be any bicartesian closed category and let
F
be a finite-product-preserving functor from
\mathit{Cl}
to
\mathds{D}
. Now, given any model
W
of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
) in
\mathit{Cl}
, we can define a model
F(W)
of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
) in
\mathds{D}
as follows:

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)}

\displaystyle\triangleq F(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W})

\displaystyle\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)}

\displaystyle\triangleq\Pi\llbracket A_{i}\rrbracket_{F(W)}(=\Pi F(\llbracket A_{i}\rrbracket_{W}))\xrightarrow{p^{-1}}F(\Pi\llbracket A_{i}\rrbracket_{W})\xrightarrow{F(\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket_{W})}F(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W})

where
\Gamma=x_{1}:A_{1},x_{2}:A_{2},\ldots,x_{n}:A_{n}
and
p
is a witness of the finite-product-preserving property of
F
.

Next, we check that
F(W)
is indeed a model of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
), then
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathit{Cl}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{W},\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W})
.

Therefore,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{D}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{F(W)},\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)})
. Hence,
F(W)
is a well-defined model of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

Now, say
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

Then,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W}=\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathit{Cl}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{W},\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W})
.

So,
F(\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W})\circ q=F(\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W})\circ q\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{D}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{F(W)},\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)})
.

Or,
\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)}=\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{D}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket_{F(W)},\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(W)})
.

Hence,
F(W)
is a sound model of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

Next, we show the universal property: for any given model
W^{\prime}
of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
) in any bicartesian closed category
\mathds{D}
, there exists a
unique
finite-product-preserving functor,
F
, from
\mathit{Cl}
to
\mathds{D}
such that
F(\mathit{G})=W^{\prime}
.

Given
W^{\prime}
, we define a functor,
F
, from
\mathit{Cl}
to
\mathds{D}
, as follows:

\displaystyle F(A)

\displaystyle\triangleq\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}

\displaystyle F(A\xrightarrow{a}B)

\displaystyle\triangleq\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{a}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}

Since
A\xrightarrow{a}B
in
\mathit{Cl}
, we know that
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

Therefore,
\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{a}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
). So,
\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{a}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{D}}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}},\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}})
.

Here, we need to check that the definition of
F
respects the equivalence relation on morphisms in
\mathit{Cl}
. In other words, we need to check that if
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
), then
\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}=\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}
. But this is true because
W^{\prime}
is a sound model of GMCC(
\mathcal{M}
).

So
F
is well-defined.

Next, we check that
F
is indeed a functor.

F(A\xrightarrow{\text{id}}A)=\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\xrightarrow{\text{id}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}

\displaystyle F(A\xrightarrow{f}B\xrightarrow{g}C)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{g}\>\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{C}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{g}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}},\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{g}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}},\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}},\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\emptyset\vdash\mathit{g}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\circ\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\emptyset\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\circ\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\emptyset\vdash\mathit{g}:\mathit{B}\to\mathit{C}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\circ\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\emptyset\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\circ\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{y}:\mathit{B}\vdash\mathit{g}\>\mathit{y}:\mathit{C}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\circ\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{f}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle F(B\xrightarrow{g}C)\circ F(A\xrightarrow{f}B)

Observe that
F(\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\times\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}})=\llbracket\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\times\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}=\llbracket\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}\times\llbracket\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}=F\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\times F\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Hence,
F
is a finite-product-preserving functor.

Now, we show that
F(\mathit{G})=W^{\prime}
.

\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(\mathit{G})}=F(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{\mathit{G}})=F(A)=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}.

Further,

\displaystyle\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{F(\mathit{G})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle F(\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{\mathit{G}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle F(\lambda x.a\{\text{proj}_{i}^{n}\>x/x_{i}\})\hskip 6.0pt[\text{Here},\Gamma=x_{1}:A_{1},x_{2}:A_{2},\ldots,x_{n}:A_{n}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket x:\Pi A_{i}\vdash a\{\text{proj}_{i}^{n}\>x/x_{i}\}:A\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}.

Therefore,
F(\mathit{G})=W^{\prime}
.

Next, we show the uniqueness property. Let
F^{\prime}
be a finite-product-preserving functor from
\mathit{Cl}
to
\mathds{D}
such that
F^{\prime}(\mathit{G})=W^{\prime}
. Need to show that
F^{\prime}=F
.

Now,
F^{\prime}(A)=F^{\prime}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{\mathit{G}})=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}=F(A)
.

Also,
F^{\prime}(A\xrightarrow{a}B)=F^{\prime}(\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{a}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{\mathit{G}})=\llbracket\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{a}\>\mathit{x}:\mathit{B}\rrbracket_{W^{\prime}}=F(A\xrightarrow{a}B)
.

Therefore,
F^{\prime}=F
.

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix F
Proofs of lemmas/theorems stated in Section
8

Theorem F.1 (Theorem
8.1
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\overline{\Gamma}\vdash\overline{\mathit{a}}:\overline{\mathit{A}}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
). Further, if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\equiv\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\simeq\overline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
).

Proof.

Follows from Theorems
B.1
and
D.1
.
∎

Lemma F.2 (Lemma
8.2
).

If
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
, then there exists a term
\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}
such that
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Prod
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\exists

\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}
and
\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}
such that
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
and
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

Now,

\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{z}.(j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}),j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})):S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}})\to\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}}\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}})\vdash(j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}),j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})):\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}}\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}})\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}):\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}})\vdash(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\emptyset\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}):S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}})\to S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}:\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}

Note that we omit the derivation of
\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}})\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}):\underline{\mathit{B}}
, which is similar to that of
\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}})\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}):\underline{\mathit{A}}
.

Therefore,
j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}}}=\lambda\mathit{z}.(j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}),j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}))
and
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Fun
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\exists

\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}
such that
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

Now,

\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{z}.\lambda\mathit{y}.j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}):S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}})\to(\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}})\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}),\mathit{y}:\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}):\underline{\mathit{B}}\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}),\mathit{y}:\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}\mathit{y}:\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}):S_{\ell}\>(\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}})\to S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}\mathit{y}:\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}:(\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}})\to\underline{\mathit{B}}

Therefore,
j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}}=\lambda\mathit{z}.\lambda\mathit{y}.j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})
and
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Monad
. Have:
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq T_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Now,
\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}\to S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}
.

Therefore,
j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}
and
\lfloor j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Already
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq T_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\exists

\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}
such that
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

Now,

\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell^{\prime}}j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x})):S_{\ell}\>S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell}\>S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell^{\prime}}j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x})):S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\lx@proof@logical@and\emptyset\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell^{\prime}}j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell}\>S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{fork}^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x}):S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell}\>S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell\vee\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}

Notice the flip going from
\mathbf{join}
to
\mathbf{fork}
in the above derivation!

Therefore,
j^{\ell}_{S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell^{\prime}}j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x}))
and
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Minimum
. Have:
\bot\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
.

Now,
\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x}:S_{\bot}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}
.

Therefore,
j^{\bot}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x}
and
\lfloor j^{\bot}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Combine
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
where
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By IH,
\exists

\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}
and
\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}
such that
\lfloor j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
and
\lfloor j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

Now,

\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}.j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}))):S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}))):\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})):S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\lx@proof@logical@and\emptyset\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}):S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}

Therefore,
j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})))
and
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

∎

Theorem F.3 (Theorem
8.3
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash\underline{\mathit{a}}:\underline{\mathit{A}}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{L}
). Further, if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
such that
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\simeq\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\underline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\simeq\underline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{L}
).

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. By IH.

•

Rule
DCC-Eta
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash\underline{\mathit{a}}:\underline{\mathit{A}}
.

Next,
\underline{\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}}=\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell}(\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\mathit{a}})
.

Now,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\mathit{a}}:S_{\bot}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}
.

So,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell}(\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\mathit{a}}):S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}
.

•

Rule
DCC-Bind
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash\underline{\mathit{a}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}
and
\underline{\Gamma},\mathit{x}:\underline{\mathit{A}}\vdash\underline{\mathit{b}}:\underline{\mathit{B}}
.

Now, since
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
, by Lemma
F.2
,
\exists

\emptyset\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}
such that
\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\equiv\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}
.

Next,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}:\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}
.

So,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}):S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}\to S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}
.

As such,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}))\>\underline{\mathit{a}}:S_{\ell}\>\underline{\mathit{B}}
.

Then,
\underline{\Gamma}\vdash j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}))\>\underline{\mathit{a}}):\underline{\mathit{B}}
.

Now, we can show that, if
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\lfloor\underline{\mathit{a}}\rfloor\equiv\lfloor\mathit{a}\rfloor
. The proof is by straightforward induction on the typing judgement. For the
\mathbf{eta}
-case, note that
\lfloor\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell}(\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\mathit{a}})\rfloor=\lfloor\underline{\mathit{a}}\rfloor
. For the
\mathbf{bind}
-case, note that

\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}))\>\underline{\mathit{a}})\rfloor=\lfloor j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rfloor\>((\lambda\mathit{x}.\lfloor\underline{\mathit{b}}\rfloor)\>\lfloor\underline{\mathit{a}}\rfloor)\equiv(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y})\>((\lambda\mathit{x}.\lfloor\underline{\mathit{b}}\rfloor)\>\lfloor\underline{\mathit{a}}\rfloor)\equiv\lfloor\underline{\mathit{b}}\rfloor\{\lfloor\underline{\mathit{a}}\rfloor/\mathit{x}\}
.

So, for
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{A}
, if
\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\simeq\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
), then we have:

\lfloor\underline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\rfloor\equiv\lfloor\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rfloor\equiv\lfloor\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\rfloor\equiv\lfloor\underline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\rfloor.

Hence,
\underline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\simeq\underline{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{L}
).

∎

Theorem F.4 (Theorem
8.4
).

Let
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
be any derivation in GMCC(
\mathcal{L}
). Then,
\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}}\equiv\mathit{a}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
. Note that
\underline{\overline{\mathit{A}}}=\mathit{A}
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. By IH.

•

Rule
MC-Return
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}:S_{1}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}}\equiv\mathit{a}
. Now,

\underline{\overline{\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}}}=\underline{\mathbf{eta}^{\bot}\overline{\mathit{a}}}=\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\bot}(\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}})\equiv\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}}\equiv\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}.

•

Rule
MC-Fmap
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}\mathit{f}:S_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}\to S_{\ell}\>\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\underline{\overline{\mathit{f}}}\equiv\mathit{f}
. Now,

\displaystyle\underline{\overline{\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}\mathit{f}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underline{\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}.\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.j^{\ell}_{S_{\ell}\>\mathit{B}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\underline{\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y})}))\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell}(\mathbf{ret}\>(\underline{\overline{\mathit{f}}}\>\mathit{y}))))\>\mathit{x})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 3.84726pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 3.84726pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 7.05984pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 8.52473pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\ell}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell}(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{f}\>\mathit{y})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell}(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 3.84726pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 3.84726pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 7.05984pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 8.52473pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\bot}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{y}))\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{eq:natr})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 3.84726pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 3.84726pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 7.05984pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}{\kern 8.52473pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\bot}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathit{f}\>\mathit{y})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}\mathit{f}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{lifteqn})]

•

Rule
MC-Join
. Have
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}}\equiv\mathit{a}
. Now,

\displaystyle\underline{\overline{\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underline{\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y}}))\>\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\underline{\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{y}}))\>\mathit{x})))\>\mathit{a})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{x})))\>\mathit{a})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{y})))))\>\mathit{a})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\bot}\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{a})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{eq:natr})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}))\>\mathit{a})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{eq:idr})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{eq:natr})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\equiv\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{joineqn})]

•

Rule
MC-Up
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By IH,
\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}}\equiv\mathit{a}
. Now,

\displaystyle\underline{\overline{\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underline{\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}}))\>\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x})))\>\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}))

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\bot}\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{eq:natr})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Equation }(\ref{eq:idr})]

•

Rule
MC-Extract
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:S_{1}\>\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}}\equiv\mathit{a}
.

Now,
\underline{\overline{\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{a}}}=\underline{\mathbf{bind}^{\bot}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{x}}=j^{\bot}_{\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\bot}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}))\>\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}})\equiv j^{\bot}_{\mathit{A}}\>\mathit{a}=\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{a}
.

•

Rule
MC-Fork
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}}\equiv\mathit{a}
. Now,

\displaystyle\underline{\overline{\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underline{\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}}))\>\underline{\overline{\mathit{a}}})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}))\>\mathit{a})

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{Say, }\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\triangleq(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}))\>\mathit{a}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{0}}}}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{z}))))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{0}}}}))

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y})))\>\mathit{z})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{0}}}}))\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:ccomp})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y})))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{0}}}})))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{t_{{\mathrm{0}}}}))

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{f})\>\mathit{a})))

\displaystyle[\text{Say, }\mathit{f}\triangleq\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}((\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{f}\>(\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 9.49045pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 10.95534pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a})))

\displaystyle[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{lifteq2})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y}))\>((\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{f}\>(\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 9.49045pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 10.95534pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:cassoc})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\lambda\mathit{z}.(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y}))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})))\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle[\because(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{f}\>(\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x}))\>\mathchoice{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 9.49045pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 10.95534pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}\equiv\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})\;(\text{See below})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}))\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a})))

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:ccomp})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.((\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\bot}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}))))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:cnatl})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{extr}\>(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\bot}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}))))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>(\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}))))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:cidl})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.(\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z})))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z}))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\bot}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:natr})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\gg}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\gg\!\!=^{\bot}\lambda\mathit{z}.\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{z}\equiv\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:idr})]

Note that:

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{f}\>(\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{x})\>\mathchoice{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 9.49045pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 10.95534pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 9.49045pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 10.95534pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:cnatr})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{x}))\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{y})\>\mathchoice{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 6.75002pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 9.49045pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}{\kern 10.95534pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\bot}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{lifteq2})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathit{y}\>\mathchoice{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 7.75009pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 10.96268pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}{\kern 12.42757pt\mathop{}\mathopen{\vphantom{\ll}}^{\mathmakebox[0pt][l]{\mathmakebox[r]{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}}}\ll\!\!=^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:cnatr})]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{z})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Eqn. }(\ref{eq:cidn})]

∎

Appendix G
Proofs of lemmas/theorems stated in Section
9

Lemma G.1.

If
\ell\sqsubseteq A
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
, then
\exists

k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
such that
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Prod
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\exists k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
and
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)
such that

k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
and
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
.

Define:
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}=\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)\xrightarrow{\langle\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1},\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\rangle}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket
.

Need to show:
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
.

This equation follows from the commutative diagram in Figure
29
. The diagram in Figure
29
commutes: the triangle commutes by naturality; the square too commutes by naturality; the circular segment commutes by IH.

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\bot}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\langle\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\pi_{1},\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\pi_{2}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\langle\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1},\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}
