.
Commutative diagram

•

Rule
Prot-Fun
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\exists k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)
such that
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
.

Define:
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}=\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\xrightarrow{t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\xrightarrow{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)\xrightarrow{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket\Big)
.

Need to show:
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
.

Now,

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\Big)\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}})\times\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\Big)\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Figure \ref{prfcd7}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda(\text{app})\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By IH}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}

Note that in Figure
30
,
\overline{\eta}_{\mathit{X}}:=\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\mathit{X}}\circ\eta_{\mathit{X}}
. The diagram in this figure commutes by naturality.

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}

\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times\overline{\eta}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}}
id
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{\eta}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}}

app

\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}}

Rule
Prot-Monad
. Have
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Define:
k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

Need to show:
k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

Now,

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By lax monoidality}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By naturality}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}[\because\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}]=\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

•

Rule
Prot-Already
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\exists k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
such that

k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

Define:
k^{\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell\vee\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket=\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}\vee\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket
.

Need to show:
k^{\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

This equation follows from the commutative diagram in Figure
31
. The diagram in Figure
31
commutes: the left triangle in the top row commutes because
\mathbf{S}
is a lax monoidal functor; the circular segment in the top row commutes because
\mathbf{S}
is an oplax monoidal functor; the left square in the bottom row commutes because
\mu
is natural in its first component; the right square in the bottom row commutes because
\delta
is natural in its second component; the triangle to the right in the bottom row commutes by IH.

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell\vee\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{\bot,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\bot}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}^{\ell^{\prime}\sqsubseteq\ell\vee\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\epsilon_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}

•

Rule
Prot-Minimum
. Have:
\bot\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
.

Define:
k^{\bot}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\epsilon_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
. Note,
\epsilon_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\bot}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Combine
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
where
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By IH,
\exists k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
and
k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
such that:

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}.

Define:
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\mathbf{S}^{\ell\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket
.

Now,

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\ell\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\because\mathbf{S}\text{ is a functor}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\because\mathbf{S}\text{ is a functor}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By commutative diagram in Figure \ref{prfcd6}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By IH}]

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\bot}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\epsilon_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}

Figure 32
.
Commutative diagram

The diagram in Figure
32
commutes: the circular segment commutes because
\mathbf{S}
is an oplax monoidal functor; the square commutes because
\delta
is natural in its second component; the triangle commutes by IH.

∎

Lemma G.2.

If
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
, then
\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

Proof.

Let
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
. By Lemma
G.1
, we know that
\exists k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
such that
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
.

Now,

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle\text{or, }\mathbf{S}_{\ell}k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\because\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{ is a functor}]

\displaystyle\text{or, }\mathbf{S}_{\ell}k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\because(\mathbf{S}_{\ell},\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}\circ\eta,\mu^{\ell,\ell})\text{ is an idempotent monad}]

\displaystyle\text{or, }\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle=\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By naturality of }\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}\circ\eta]

This shows that
\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\cong\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket
.

∎

Lemma G.3 (Lemma
9.1
).

If
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
, then
\exists
an isomorphism
k:\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\to\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket
.

Further,
k\circ\overline{\eta}=id_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
and
\overline{\eta}\circ k=\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
where
\overline{\eta}\triangleq\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}\circ\eta:\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\to\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket
.

Proof.

Follows by lemma
G.1
and
G.2
.
∎

Theorem G.4 (Theorem
9.2
).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
, then
\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. Standard.

•

Rule
DCC-Eta
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
.

Therefore,
\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)
.

•

Rule
DCC-Bind
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
G.1
,
\exists k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)
. Now,

\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\rrbracket=\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\xrightarrow{\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle}\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket)\xrightarrow{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket}\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket\xrightarrow{k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket
.

Therefore,
\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\rrbracket\in\text{Hom}_{\mathds{C}}(\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)
.

∎

Theorem G.5 (Theorem
9.3
).

If
\Gamma\vdash a:A
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
and
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
, then
\llbracket a\rrbracket=\llbracket a\textquoteright\rrbracket
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. Standard.

•

Rule
DCC-Eta
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

Further,
\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion on
\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{c}
.

–

Rule
CBV-Eta
. Have:
\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a^{\prime}}
where
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a^{\prime}}\rrbracket
.

Therefore,
\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a^{\prime}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a^{\prime}}\rrbracket
.

•

Rule
DCC-Bind
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

Further,
\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion on
\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
.

–

Rule
CBV-BindLeft
. Have:
\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}
where
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a^{\prime}}\rrbracket
.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a^{\prime}}\rrbracket\rangle=\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\rrbracket.

–

Rule
CBV-BindBeta
. Have:
\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{v}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b}\{\mathit{v}/\mathit{x}\}
.

Now,

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{v}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ(\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}\times\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\bot}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\because\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}\text{ is strong}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\bot}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ(\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket}\times\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\because\eta\text{ is strong}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ\eta_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By naturality}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By naturality}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Lemma \ref{Ap2}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathit{b}\{\mathit{v}/\mathit{x}\}\rrbracket

∎

Theorem G.6 (Theorem
9.4
).

Let the interpretation
\llbracket\_\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
be injective for ground types.

•

Let
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathbf{Bool}
and
\mathit{v}
be a value of type
\mathbf{Bool}
. If
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
, then
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto^{\ast}\mathit{v}
.

•

Fix some
\ell\in L
. Let
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathbf{Bool}
and
\mathit{v}
be a value of type
\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathbf{Bool}
. Suppose, the morphisms
\overline{\eta}_{X}\triangleq\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\mathit{X}}\circ\eta_{\mathit{X}}
are mono for any
X\in\text{Obj}(\mathds{C})
. Now, if
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
, then
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto^{\ast}\mathit{v}
.

Proof.

Let
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathbf{Bool}
and
\mathit{v}
be a boolean value such that
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
.

We show that
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto^{\ast}\mathit{v}
.

First note that
\text{DCC}_{e}
is strongly normalizing with respect to the reduction relation,
\leadsto^{\ast}
. Further,
\text{DCC}_{e}
is also type sound with respect to this reduction relation.

Therefore, given
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathbf{Bool}
, we know that there exists a value
\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
such that
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}:\mathbf{Bool}
and
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto^{\ast}\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
.

Next, by Theorem
G.5
,
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
.

Since
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
(given) and
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
(above), therefore,
\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
.

But, by injectivity,
\mathit{v}=\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
.

Thus,
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto^{\ast}\mathit{v}
.

For the second part, we use a similar argument.

Given
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathbf{Bool}
, we know that there exists a value
\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
such that
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathbf{Bool}
and
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto^{\ast}\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
.

By Theorem
G.5
,
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
.

Since
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
(given) and
\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
(above), therefore,
\llbracket\mathit{v}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
.

Both
\mathit{v}
and
\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
are values of type
\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathbf{Bool}
. Therefore,
\mathit{v}=\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{v^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}=\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{v^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
, for some values
\mathit{v^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{v^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
of type
\mathbf{Bool}
.

Then,
\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{v^{\prime}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{v^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
, or
\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathbf{Bool}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathbf{Bool}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{v^{\prime}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathbf{Bool}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathbf{Bool}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{v^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
.

Since for any
X\in\text{Obj}(\mathds{C})
,
\overline{\eta}_{\mathit{X}}
is mono, so
\llbracket\mathit{v^{\prime}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}=\llbracket\mathit{v^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\rrbracket_{(\mathds{C},\mathbf{S})}
.

By injectivity,
\mathit{v^{\prime}}=\mathit{v^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
.

Therefore,
\mathit{v}=\mathit{v_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
.

Hence,
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto^{\ast}\mathit{v}
.
∎

Theorem G.7.

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in GMCC(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
. Note that
\llbracket\overline{\mathit{A}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. By IH.

•

Rule
MC-Return
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}:S_{1}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

Now,
\overline{\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}}=\mathbf{eta}^{\bot}\overline{\mathit{a}}
.

Then,
\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\bot}\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\bot}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathbf{ret}\>\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

•

Rule
MC-Extract
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:S_{1}\>\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

Now,
\overline{\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{a}}=\mathbf{bind}^{\bot}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{x}
.

Then,

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\bot}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{x}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\bot}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\pi_{2}\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\bot}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\bot}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\because\mathbf{S}^{\bot}\text{ is strong}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\epsilon_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Lemma \ref{Ap2}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{extr}\>\mathit{a}\rrbracket

Rule
MC-Fmap
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}\mathit{f}:S_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}\to S_{\ell}\>\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\overline{\mathit{f}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket
.

Now,
\overline{\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}\mathit{f}}=\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}.\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y})
.

Then,

\displaystyle\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\overline{\mathit{A}}.\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{y}=\mathit{x}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(k^{\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}(\overline{\mathit{f}}\>\mathit{y})\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket},\pi_{2}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(k^{\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ(\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket\times\text{id}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\langle\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{1},\pi_{2}\rangle)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket},\pi_{2}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(k^{\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{1},\pi_{2}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket},\pi_{2}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(k^{\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{1},\pi_{2}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket},\pi_{2}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt[\text{By idempotence}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{1},\pi_{2}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket},\pi_{2}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt[\text{By Theorem \ref{Ap2}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\mathit{f}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big)\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By commutative diagram \ref{prfcd8}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}\mathit{f}\rrbracket

Rule
MC-Fork
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
.

\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

\overline{\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}}=\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}
.

Then,

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\rrbracket

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2})\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\because\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\text{ is strong}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt[\text{By Lemma \ref{Ap2}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Lemma \ref{Ap2}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Lemma \ref{Ap2}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mu^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\bot}\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\delta^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Figure \ref{prfcd9}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Figure \ref{prfcd10}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\rrbracket

The diagram in Figure
35
commutes: all the squares commute by naturality; the ellipses commute because
\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mu^{\bot,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\bot}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\epsilon_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
; the circular segment commutes by lax monoidality. The diagram in Figure
36
commutes because
\mathbf{S}
is a strong monoidal functor.

Rule
MC-Up
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:S_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{A}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\overline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

Now,
\overline{\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}}=\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}
.

Then,

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\mathit{x}=\overline{\mathit{a}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2})\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2})\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Theorem \ref{Ap2}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\pi_{2}\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\pi_{2}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\because\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\text{ is strong}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By naturality}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\because(\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\circ\eta,\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\text{ is a monad}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{up}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{a}\rrbracket

∎

Theorem G.8.

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
(
\mathcal{L}
), then
\llbracket\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
. Note that
\llbracket\underline{\mathit{A}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket
.

•

\lambda
-calculus. By IH.

•

Rule
DCC-Eta
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
.

Now,
\underline{\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}}=\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell}(\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\mathit{a}})
.

Then,

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{up}^{\bot,\ell}(\mathbf{ret}\>\underline{\mathit{a}})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}^{\bot\sqsubseteq\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\eta_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By IH}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{eta}^{\ell}\mathit{a}\rrbracket

•

Rule
DCC-Bind
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:\mathcal{T}_{\ell}\>\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:\mathit{B}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\llbracket\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket
and
\llbracket\underline{\mathit{b}}\rrbracket=\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket
.

Now,
\underline{\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}}=j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}))\>\underline{\mathit{a}})
.

Then,

\displaystyle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}))\>\underline{\mathit{a}})\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}))\>\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}})\rrbracket,\llbracket\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}.\underline{\mathit{b}}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\llbracket\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\underline{\mathit{b}}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\llbracket\underline{\mathit{a}}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big),\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By IH}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\rangle\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{b}\rrbracket\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\text{id}_{\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket},\llbracket\mathit{a}\rrbracket\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By Lemma \ref{jk}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\mathbf{bind}^{\ell}\>\mathit{x}=\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\rrbracket

∎

Lemma G.9.

If
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
in
\text{DCC}_{e}
, then
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle
.

Proof.

By induction on
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
.

•

Rule
Prot-Prod
. Have
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}\times\mathit{B}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle
and
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\rangle
.

Now,
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}=k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\times k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1},\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\rangle
.

Next,
j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}\times\underline{\mathit{B}}}=\lambda\mathit{z}.(j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}),j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}))
.

Then,

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{z}.(j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}),j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}))\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})\rrbracket,\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})\rrbracket\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket\rangle,\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket,k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By IH}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y})\rrbracket,\pi_{2}\rangle,k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y})\rrbracket,\pi_{2}\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{1}\>\mathit{y}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\mathit{X},\mathit{Y}}\Big),\pi_{2}\rangle,k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{y}.\mathbf{proj}_{2}\>\mathit{y}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\mathit{X},\mathit{Y}}\Big),\pi_{2}\rangle\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle\hskip 5.0pt[\mathit{X}:=\top\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket)\text{ and }\mathit{Y}:=\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{2})\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\mathit{X},\mathit{Y}}\Big),\pi_{2}\rangle,k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{2})\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\mathit{X},\mathit{Y}}\Big),\pi_{2}\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{2}),\pi_{2}\rangle,k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{2}),\pi_{2}\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\Lambda(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{2})\times\text{id}\circ\langle\text{id},\pi_{2}\rangle,k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\text{app}\circ\Lambda(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{2})\times\text{id}\circ\langle\text{id},\pi_{2}\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1}\circ\pi_{2}\circ\langle\text{id},\pi_{2}\rangle,k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{2}\circ\langle\text{id},\pi_{2}\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1},k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\pi_{2}\circ\langle\text{id},\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{1},k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\pi_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket\times\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}

Rule
Prot-Fun
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\rangle
.

Now,
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}=\Lambda(k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle)
.

And
j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}\to\underline{\mathit{B}}}=\lambda\mathit{z}.\lambda\mathit{y}.j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})
.

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{z}.\lambda\mathit{y}.j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket\lambda\mathit{y}.j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z})\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket\rangle\Big)\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\text{app}\circ(\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\times\text{id})\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\llbracket(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}))\>\mathit{z}\rrbracket\rangle\Big)\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{\ell}(\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y})\rrbracket,\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1}\rangle\rangle\Big)\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathit{x}\>\mathit{y}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{(\top\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\Big),\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1}\rangle\rangle\Big)\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell}(\text{app}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1}\rangle)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{(\top\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\Big),\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1}\rangle\rangle\Big)\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1},\pi_{2}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{(\top\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket})\times\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\text{id},\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1}\rangle\rangle\Big)\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1},\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\langle\text{id},\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1}\rangle\rangle\Big)\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By naturality}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{1}\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\langle\rangle,\pi_{1}\rangle,\pi_{2}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda\Big(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{B}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\text{app}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}\circ\langle\pi_{2},\pi_{1}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{B}\rrbracket^{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}}

Rule
Prot-Monad
. Have:
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Now,
k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
and
j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}
.

Then,
\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}.\mathbf{join}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle=\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle=\mu^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

Rule
Prot-Already
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathcal{T}_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\mathit{A}
where
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle
.

Now,
k^{\ell}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}
and
j^{\ell}_{S_{\ell^{\prime}}\>\underline{\mathit{A}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell^{\prime}}j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x}))
.

Then,

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}.(\mathbf{lift}^{\ell^{\prime}}j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x}))\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{\ell^{\prime}}j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket,\llbracket\mathbf{fork}^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}(\mathbf{join}^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{x})\rrbracket\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}}_{\top,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big)\circ\langle\rangle,\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle\circ\pi_{2})\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}}_{\top,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt[\because\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}\rangle\circ\pi_{2}=\text{id}_{\top\times\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle)\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\pi_{2}\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}}_{\top,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\pi_{2}\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}}_{\top,\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By IH}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\delta^{\ell^{\prime},\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mu^{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\mathbf{S}_{\ell^{\prime}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle\hskip 3.0pt[\text{By strength}]

\bot\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}

Rule
Prot-Combine
. Have:
\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
where
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By IH,
k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle
and
k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

Now,
k^{\ell}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}=k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}k^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\ell\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}

and
j^{\ell}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}.j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})))
.

Then,

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket\lambda\mathit{x}.j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})))\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\llbracket j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\>((\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}})\>(\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})))\rrbracket\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket\mathbf{lift}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\llbracket\mathbf{fork}^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}(\mathbf{up}^{\ell,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{x})\rrbracket\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{app}\circ\langle\llbracket j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket\circ\langle\rangle,\text{app}\circ\langle\Lambda\Big(\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}(\Lambda^{-1}\llbracket j^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\underline{\mathit{A}}}\rrbracket)\circ t^{\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}_{\top,\mathbf{S}_{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\Big)\circ\langle\rangle,\delta^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\mathbf{S}^{\ell\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vee\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}_{\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\circ\pi_{2}\rangle\rangle\circ\langle\langle\rangle,\text{id}_{\mathbf{S}_{\ell}\llbracket\mathit{A}\rrbracket}\rangle

\displaystyle=
